- **chemical-exposure-register**: Build an OEL/WEL/PEL-linked chemical hazard and exposure register, organised by similar-exposure group (SEG), banding each exposure with the risk matrix and flagging where health surveillance or air monitoring is due. Use it to build a chemical exposure register, link agents to occupational exposure limits, assess inhalation/dermal exposure risk by SEG, or plan a monitoring schedule. Every limit is cited with its source and year; controls are hierarchy-of-controls ranked; worker exposure data is de-identified to role/SEG labels before drafting.
